DG-Hard also restores safety alignment degraded by benign fine-tuning on three independent safety axes, despite using no alignment data. These results suggest that part of fine-tuning-induced capability loss is not an unavoidable consequence of specialization, but a removable spectral residue in the weight update itself.

</details>


### [122] [Mechanisms of Misgeneralization in Physical Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20299)
*Kento Nishi,Raphael Tang,Karun Kumar,Core Francisco Park,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准深度学习模型在生成序列轨迹时，虽然单条轨迹看起来合理，但聚合后的物理量分布（如行驶距离、机械能）会偏离训练数据所指定的分布，这种被称为"物理泛化错误"的现象源于模型类的局部误差通过物理测量传播，导致分布偏移。作者通过数据偏差核来预测哪些物理量会增减，并提出核信息干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人技术和机械模拟等物理领域，工程师通过精心策划演示数据来指定轨迹在物理量（如行驶距离、机械能）上的分布，但标准深度学习方法会违反这一意图，导致聚合分布错误。

Method: 通过可控合成任务建立物理泛化错误机制的解释，使用数据偏差核估计局部误差并预测物理量的分布偏移，并在迷宫导航和双摆运动任务中验证。

Result: 发现物理泛化错误源于模型类的局部误差通过物理测量传播，导致恢复的分布发生偏移；数据偏差核可以预测哪些物理量会增重或减重。

Conclusion: 基于机制解释识别出有前景的缓解策略，并提出核信息干预方法。

Abstract: Generative sequence models are often trained to plan motion in physical domains, from robotics to mechanical simulations. When constructing a dataset to train such a model, engineers may curate demonstrations to specify how trajectories should be distributed over a physical quantity like travel distance or mechanical energy. For example, a roboticist building a maze navigation agent might choose demonstrations whose travel distances cover a fixed range uniformly, hoping to constrain the agent's expected power usage. We find that standard deep learning can violate this intent: each generated trajectory can seem plausible on its own, but the aggregate distribution over the physical quantity is wrong. We call this failure physical misgeneralization, and develop an account of its mechanism. Using controlled synthetic tasks, we show that physical misgeneralization arises when local errors typical of the model class propagate through the physical measurement to shift the recovered distribution. We estimate these errors with a data deviation kernel, and we use it to predict which physical quantities gain or lose mass in both our synthetic and more applied maze navigation and double-pendulum motion tasks. Finally, our mechanistic interpretation helps identify which mitigation strategies are structurally promising, and we use it to propose a kernel-informed intervention.

</details>


### [123] [Robust Subspace-Constrained Quadratic Models for Low-Dimensional Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20300)
*Zheng Zhai,Xiaohui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的子空间约束二次模型（SCQM），用于从高维数据中学习低维结构。该模型在SQMF框架基础上扩展，能处理包括广义高斯和径向拉普拉斯在内的多种噪声分布，在重尾和轻尾噪声下均表现可靠。采用基于梯度的回溯线搜索算法求解非凸优化问题，并通过敏感性分析阐明了ℓ_p^p和ℓ_2损失函数在不同噪声特性下的行为。实验表明该方法在鲁棒性和重构精度上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子空间约束二次矩阵分解（SQMF）框架对噪声分布的假设较为局限，难以同时适应重尾和轻尾噪声场景，缺乏对更广泛噪声分布类型的鲁棒性支持。

Method: 提出鲁棒子空间约束二次模型（SCQM），扩展SQMF框架以容纳广义高斯和径向拉普拉斯等噪声分布；设计基于梯度的回溯线搜索算法求解非凸优化问题；对ℓ_p^p和ℓ_2损失函数进行敏感性分析。

Result: 数值实验验证了理论分析，所提方法在鲁棒性和重构精度上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SCQM模型通过泛化噪声分布假设有效提升了子空间学习的鲁棒性，在不同噪声特性下均能保持稳定性能，是一种更具普适性的低维结构学习方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a robust subspace-constrained quadratic model (SCQM) for learning low-dimensional structure from high-dimensional data. Building upon the subspace-constrained quadratic matrix factorization (SQMF) framework, the proposed model accommodates a broad class of noise distributions, including generalized Gaussian and radial Laplace models. This generalization enables reliable performance under both heavy-tailed and light-tailed noise, thereby substantially enhancing robustness across diverse data regimes. To efficiently address the resulting nonconvex optimization problem, we develop a gradient-based algorithm equipped with a backtracking line-search strategy that ensures stable and efficient convergence. In addition, we present a sensitivity analysis of the $\ell_p^p$ and $\ell_2$ loss functions, elucidating their distinct behaviors under varying noise characteristics. Extensive numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical analysis and demonstrate that the proposed approach consistently outperforms existing methods in terms of robustness and reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


### [124] [Neural Collapse by Design: Learning Class Prototypes on the Hypersphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20302)
*Panagiotis Koromilas,Theodoros Giannakopoulos,Mihalis A. Nicolaou,Yannis Panagakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明交叉熵（CE）和监督对比学习（SCL）本质上是同一种方法——超球面上的原型对比，并提出NTCE和NONL两种归一化损失来修复CE的缺陷，同时证明SCL的线性探测阶段是冗余且有害的，应直接使用类均值嵌入作为分类器。实验表明，新方法在ImageNet-1K等基准上超越CE精度，接近神经坍缩（NC）最优几何，且大幅加速训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有监督分类存在理论最优解（神经坍缩NC），但CE和SCL两种主流范式在实践中均无法达到：CE未约束径向自由度，收敛到退化几何；SCL在预训练阶段驱动特征趋近NC，但后续线性探测阶段又丢弃了该结构。

Method: 1) 从CE角度提出NTCE和NONL两种归一化损失，引入对比学习的关键要素（大有效负样本集、解耦的对齐与均匀性项）；2) 从SCL角度证明其目标函数已隐式优化了一个原则性分类器（权重为类均值嵌入），线性探测是冗余且有害的，应直接使用固定原型。

Result: 在四个基准（含ImageNet-1K）上：NTCE和NONL超越CE精度，接近NC（≥95%），在4/5指标上仅用CE 7.5%的迭代次数即达到其NC水平；SCL固定原型匹配线性探测效果且无需数小时训练；迁移学习提升+5.5%，严重类别不平衡下提升+8.7%，ImageNet-C上mCE更低。

Conclusion: 有监督学习可重构为超球面上的原型学习，两条路径均可通过设计达到NC最优几何。

Abstract: Supervised classification has a theoretical optimum, Neural Collapse (NC), yet neither of its two dominant paradigms reaches it in practice. Cross entropy (CE) leaves radial degrees of freedom unconstrained and converges to a degenerate geometry, while supervised contrastive learning (SCL) drives features toward NC during pretraining but discards this structure in a post hoc linear probing phase. We show that both paradigms are different appearances of the same method, prototype contrast on the unit hypersphere, and that closing the gap requires fixing each at its specific point of failure. From the CE side, we propose NTCE and NONL, two normalized losses that import contrastive optimization's missing ingredients into classifier learning: a large effective negative set and decoupled alignment and uniformity terms. From the SCL side, we prove that SCL's objective already optimizes throughout training for a principled classifier whose weights are the class mean embeddings, making linear probing both redundant and harmful. Empirically, on four benchmarks including ImageNet-1K, NTCE and NONL surpass CE accuracy, closely approximate NC ($\geq 95\%$), and match CE's converged NC on 4/5 metrics in under $7.5\%$ of its iterations, while SCL with fixed prototypes matches linear probing without the hours-long classifier training phase. The learned geometry yields $+5.5\%$ mean relative improvement in transfer learning, up to $+8.7\%$ under severe class imbalance, and lower mCE on ImageNet-C, recasting supervised learning as prototype learning on the hypersphere, with NC reached by design on both paths.

</details>


### [125] [AirfoilGen: A valid-by-construction and performance-aware latent diffusion model for airfoil generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20303)
*Zhijie Yang,Min Tang,Qiang Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AirfoilGen，一种基于潜扩散模型的翼型生成方法，通过圆扫表示确保几何有效性，并利用条件扩散模型实现对气动性能（如升力、阻力系数）的精确控制，在超过20万翼型的数据集上训练，平均性能条件控制精度达98.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成方法在翼型设计中存在几何无效性和物理可控性不足的问题，生成的形状缺乏有效控制且难以根据气动性能进行条件生成。

Method: 首先提出圆扫表示（circle sweeping representation）约束生成过程以确保几何有效性；然后使用Transformer将翼型编码为向量嵌入，再通过条件扩散模型在潜空间中以目标气动性能为条件进行去噪生成。

Result: 在超过20万翼型的数据集上训练，平均性能条件控制精度达到98.41%，在几何有效性和气动性能可控性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: AirfoilGen实现了几何有效且性能可控的翼型生成，为深度学习在翼型设计中的应用提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Airfoil shape design is a fundamental task in aerospace engineering, with a direct impact on flight stability and fuel consumption. Deep learning has recently emerged as a promising tool for this task, but existing deep generative approaches remain limited in both geometric validity and physical controllability. They offer little control over the generated shapes, yielding invalid geometries, and they typically do not condition effectively on aerodynamic performance. To address these issues, this paper proposes AirfoilGen, a valid-by-construction and performance-aware latent diffusion model for airfoil. It first introduces a novel airfoil representation scheme, the circle sweeping representation, to constrain the generative process so that output shapes respect essential airfoil characteristics. It then enables explicit control over aerodynamic performance (e.g., lift and drag coefficients) by operating in a learned latent space: a transformer model encodes airfoil shapes into vector embeddings, and a conditional diffusion model denoises Gaussian noise into these latent embeddings while incorporating target aerodynamic performance. In addition, this paper presents a new dataset of over 200,000 airfoils, which is substantially larger than the widely used UIUC airfoil dataset (1,650 airfoils) and more suitable for training modern deep generative models. Experiments demonstrate that AirfoilGen enables airfoil generation with far greater geometric validity and aerodynamic performance controllability than previously achievable, with an average performance-conditioning accuracy of 98.41%.

</details>


### [126] [WaveGraphNet: Physics-Consistent Guided-Wave Damage Localization through Coupled Inverse-Forward Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20311)
*Vinay Sharma,Aditya Bharade,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WaveGraphNet，一种耦合逆-正向图学习框架，通过将传感器布局建模为图结构，利用正向分支作为物理一致性正则化项，实现CFRP板中导波损伤定位，在稀疏传感和有限训练数据下展现出更好的外推鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于导波的结构健康监测中，当仅有有限损伤位置可用于训练时，推断缺陷空间位置的问题约束不足，导致模型在未见区域泛化能力差。

Method: 将传感器布局显式建模为图（节点为换能器，边为传播路径）；逆分支将图结构的差分导波响应谱特征映射到损伤位置；正向分支预测候选位置的路径能量偏差模式；训练中正向分支作为物理一致性正则化器，抑制数值可行但与实测波响应能量重新分布不一致的位置估计。

Result: 所提出的图方法在稀疏导波传感中提供了强定位模型，与图和非图基线相比，在向外推至未见区域时展现出更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 耦合逆-正向图学习是在有限空间覆盖下实现导波定位的有效策略。

Abstract: Guided-wave structural health monitoring enables damage localization in composite plates using sparse networks of bonded piezoelectric transducers. However, inferring the spatial location of defects from pitch-catch measurements remains weakly constrained when only a limited set of damage locations is available for training. As a result, models trained to predict defect locations may perform well on seen cases but generalize poorly to unseen regions of the structure.
  This paper proposes WaveGraphNet, a coupled inverse--forward graph learning framework for guided-wave damage localization in Carbon Fiber Reinforced Polymer (CFRP) plates. The sensing layout is explicitly modeled as a graph, where transducers are represented as nodes and measured propagation paths define the graph connectivity. An inverse branch maps graph-structured spectral descriptors of differential guided-wave responses to a damage location, while a forward branch predicts the path-wise energy-deviation patterns of measured wave responses associated with a candidate location. During training, the forward branch serves as a physics-consistent regularizer, discouraging location estimates that are numerically plausible but inconsistent with the measured redistribution of wave-response energy. This coupling encourages agreement between inferred damage coordinates and the underlying wave propagation behavior.
  Within this benchmark, the proposed graph-based formulation provides a strong localization model for sparse guided-wave sensing and demonstrates improved robustness in extrapolation to held-out regions compared to both non-graph and graph baselines. These results highlight the potential of coupled inverse-forward graph learning as an effective strategy for guided-wave localization under limited spatial coverage.

</details>


### [127] [Less Data, Faster Training: repeating smaller datasets speeds up learning via sampling biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20314)
*Jingwen Liu,Ezra Edelman,Surbhi Goel,Bingbin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了"小数据集vs大数据集"的差距，发现在小数据集上重复训练比使用大数据集更省计算资源。这种现象在多种算法任务、架构和优化器中普遍存在，且无法用现有理论解释。作者认为加速源于采样偏差带来的逐层增长，在小数据集上更明显。理论和实验表明，小数据集多轮重复不仅是数据稀缺时的备选策略，更可主动用作优化的归纳偏好，尤其在推理任务中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究"小数据集重复训练比大数据集更省计算"这一反直觉现象的原因，填补现有理论在该问题上的解释空白。

Method: 通过理论分析和多种干预实验，从逐层增长和采样偏差的角度解释小数据集上的计算节省现象。

Result: 在小数据集上重复训练比使用大数据集可节省计算资源，该现象广泛存在于多种任务、架构和优化器中；采样偏差带来的逐层学习增长是主要原因。

Conclusion: 小数据集配合多轮重复不仅是数据稀缺时的退而求其次，更可主动作为有利的优化归纳偏好在推理任务中被利用。

Abstract: This work investigates the ``small-vs-large gap'', where repeating on fewer samples can lead to compute saving during training compared to using a larger dataset. This is observed across algorithmic tasks, architectures and optimizers and cannot be explained using prior theory. We argue that the speedup comes from appropriate layer-wise growth enabled by sampling biases, which is more pronounced when the dataset size is smaller. We provide both theoretical analysis and empirical evidence from various interventions. Our results suggest that using a smaller dataset with more repetitions is not just a fallback strategy under data scarcity, but can be proactively leveraged as a favorable inductive biases for optimization, particularly in reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [128] [Symmetrization of Loss Functions for Robust Training of Neural Networks in the Presence of Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20347)
*Alexandre Lemire Paquin,Brahim Chaib-Draa,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多类损失函数的对称化方法，推导出多类无铰链损失（multi-class unhinged loss）作为唯一凸多类对称损失，并提出了两种插值损失函数SGCE和α-MAE，在噪声标签基准上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注训练集昂贵且易出错，需要设计对标签噪声鲁棒的损失函数。对称性条件为此提供了理论保证，但现有研究多集中于二分类，多类情形下的对称损失函数设计尚不充分。

Method: 从任意多类损失函数的唯一分解（对称分量+类别不敏感项）出发，将交叉熵损失对称化得到多类无铰链损失。证明其在适当系数下是唯一凸多类对称损失，并指出它具有局部基础性作用——任何对称损失在等分量得分向量附近的线性逼近等价于该损失。进一步提出SGCE和α-MAE两种插值损失，在无铰链损失和MAE之间权衡并控制β-平滑性。

Result: 多类无铰链损失被证明是唯一满足对称性条件的凸多类损失函数。SGCE和α-MAE在标准噪声标签基准上取得了与现有鲁棒损失函数相比有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 对称化方法可有效用于设计多类噪声鲁棒损失函数，所提出的插值损失在理论和实验上均有效。

Abstract: Labeling a training set is often expensive and susceptible to errors, making the design of robust loss functions for label noise an important problem. The symmetry condition provides theoretical guarantees for robustness to such noise. In this work, we study a symmetrization method arising from the unique decomposition of any multi-class loss function into a symmetric component and a class-insensitive term. In particular, symmetrizing the cross-entropy loss leads to a linear multi-class extension of the unhinged loss. Unlike in the binary case, the multi-class version must have specific coefficients in order to satisfy the symmetry condition. Under suitable assumptions, we show that this multi-class unhinged loss is the unique convex multi-class symmetric loss. We also show that it has a fundamental local role: the linear approximation of any symmetric loss around score vectors with equal components is equivalent to the multi-class unhinged loss. We then introduce SGCE and alpha-MAE, two loss functions that interpolate between the multi-class unhinged loss and the Mean Absolute Error while allowing control of the beta-smoothness of the loss. Experiments on standard noisy-label benchmarks show competitive performance compared with existing robust loss functions.

</details>


### [129] [Consistently Informative Soft-Label Temperature for Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20357)
*Hoang-Chau Luong,Nghia Van Vo,Kaiqi Zhao,Lingwei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对知识蒸馏中固定温度策略导致的样本间软标签熵不一致、教师与学生间logit尺度强制对齐等问题，提出CIST方法，为教师和学生分别分配样本自适应的温度，使教师软标签保持信息一致性，同时放松师生间的logit尺度匹配约束，并在理论和实验上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准知识蒸馏使用固定的全局温度，但该设计是样本无关的：不同样本的logit尺度和学习难度各异，单一温度会导致教师软标签熵高度不一致——部分预测过于尖锐（缺乏类别间信息），部分过度平滑（丢失类别判别信息）；此外，师生共享相同温度强制了它们之间的logit尺度对齐，忽略了二者的容量差异。

Method: 提出CIST，为教师和学生分别分配样本自适应的温度。教师温度使软标签保持信息一致性，学生温度放松了师生间logit尺度的严格匹配；同时根据教师置信度和学生学习难度对蒸馏目标进行重加权。理论上证明教师标签熵主要由最大教师logit与温度的比值决定，为自适应平滑提供了理论依据。

Result: 在视觉和语言蒸馏任务上的实验表明，CIST缓解了固定温度带来的不一致性，在标准KD和强基线上取得一致改进，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 样本自适应的分离温度设计能有效解决固定温度在知识蒸馏中的局限性，提升软标签信息一致性并适应师生能力差异，是一种轻量且有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers knowledge from a high-capacity teacher to a compact student by matching their predictive distributions, with temperature scaling serving as a central mechanism for smoothing teacher predictions and exposing informative "dark knowledge" beyond the hard label. However, the standard fixed-temperature design is inherently sample-agnostic. Since samples differ in logit scale and learning difficulty, a single global temperature produces teacher soft labels with highly inconsistent entropy: some predictions remain overly sharp and provide limited inter-class information, whereas others become over-smoothed and lose class-discriminative information. Moreover, sharing the same temperature between teacher and student further imposes rigid logit-scale alignment despite their capacity mismatch. To address these limitations, we propose CIST (Consistently Informative Soft-label Temperature), which assigns separate sample-wise adaptive temperatures to the teacher and student. This design produces consistently informative teacher soft labels while relaxing rigid teacher--student logit-scale matching. It also reweights the distillation objective according to teacher confidence and student learning difficulty. Theoretically, we show that teacher-label entropy is largely governed by the ratio between the maximum teacher logit and the temperature, providing a principled basis for adaptive smoothing. Empirically, CIST mitigates the inconsistency induced by fixed temperature, and experiments on both vision and language distillation tasks show consistent improvements over standard KD and strong baselines with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [130] [Nonlocal operator learning for fMRI encoding and decoding tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20389)
*Andreas Kramer,Saugat Acharya,Alice Giola,Emanuele Zappala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于神经积分算子的fMRI编码-解码框架，通过非局部时空上下文建模，在开放数据集上验证了更大时间窗口和全脑记录对性能及潜在空间结构的提升作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI数据具有高维时空结构，传统方法难以有效捕捉其非局部依赖关系，亟需设计专门架构来建模大脑动力学的分布式非局部结构。

Method: 构建隐式神经积分算子框架，在辅助空间中进行不动点迭代，通过解码器完成分类和刺激预测；系统比较短/长时间窗口、视觉皮层/全脑记录对性能的影响。

Result: 更大时间窗口普遍提升性能并产生更结构化的表示；解码任务中潜在空间类间分离度优于原始数据；编码任务虽绝对性能有限但长窗口仍有一致增益。

Conclusion: 神经积分算子是建模fMRI动力学的有前景框架，更广泛的时空上下文有益于预测和表示学习，需设计专门架构以捕获大脑的分布式非局部依赖。

Abstract: Functional MRI data exhibit high-dimensional spatiotemporal structure, making both prediction and decoding challenging. In this work, we investigate neural integral-operator-based models for encoding and decoding tasks in fMRI, with particular emphasis on the role of nonlocal spatiotemporal context. We implement a latent neural integral operator framework that performs fixed point iterations in an auxiliary space from which classification and stimuli prediction is performed via a decoder. We evaluate our model on two open-source fMRI datasets.
  Our experiments examine both decoding of stimuli from fMRI recordings and encoding of fMRI dynamics from stimulus representations. A main focus is the effect of spatiotemporal context: we systematically compare short and long temporal windows, as well as the use of visual cortex vs whole brain recordings, and analyze their influence on performance and latent-space geometry. Across tasks and datasets, larger temporal windows generally improve results and produce more structured learned representations. In decoding experiments, the learned latent space often provides clearer class separation than the raw data. In encoding experiments, although absolute performance remains moderate due to the difficulty of the task, longer temporal windows still yield consistent gains.
  These findings suggest that neural integral operators provide a promising framework for modeling fMRI dynamics and that broader spatiotemporal context can be beneficial for both prediction and representation learning. More broadly, the results indicate that exploiting distributed nonlocal structure in brain dynamics requires model architectures specifically designed to capture such dependencies.

</details>


### [131] [Score-Based Causal Discovery of Latent Variable Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20396)
*Ignavier Ng,Xinshuai Dong,Haoyue Dai,Biwei Huang,Peter Spirtes,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于评分方法的潜变量因果结构学习框架，证明了评分函数在潜变量模型中的得分等价性和一致性，开发了精确和连续的评分方法，并在多种结构假设下统一了现有的基于约束的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于约束的潜变量因果发现方法存在测试顺序依赖、误差传播和显著性水平选择困难等问题，而基于评分的方法（如GES）在无潜变量设置下表现良好，但在潜变量场景下极具挑战性。因此，需要开发具有可识别性保证的评分方法。

Method: 首先证明适当设计的评分函数在潜变量因果模型中具有得分等价性和一致性；然后表征观测变量边际在不同结构假设下的自由度；据此开发精确和连续的评分方法，实现多种结构假设下的统一框架。

Result: 实验结果表明所提出的方法在潜变量因果结构学习中是有效的。

Conclusion: 本文为包含因果相关潜变量的因果结构识别提供了基于评分的方法，具有可识别性保证，并统一了多种现有基于约束的方法，为潜变量因果发现提供了新思路。

Abstract: Identifying latent variables and the causal structure involving them is essential across various scientific fields. While many existing works fall under the category of constraint-based methods (with e.g. conditional independence or rank deficiency tests), they may face empirical challenges such as testing-order dependency, error propagation, and choosing an appropriate significance level. These issues can potentially be mitigated by properly designed score-based methods, such as Greedy Equivalence Search (GES) (Chickering, 2002) in the specific setting without latent variables. Yet, formulating score-based methods with latent variables is highly challenging. In this work, we develop score-based methods that are capable of identifying causal structures containing causally-related latent variables with identifiability guarantees. Specifically, we show that a properly formulated scoring function can achieve score equivalence and consistency for structure learning of latent variable causal models. We further provide a characterization of the degrees of freedom for the marginal over the observed variables under multiple structural assumptions considered in the literature, and accordingly develop both exact and continuous score-based methods. This offers a unified view of several existing constraint-based methods with different structural assumptions. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [132] [Decomposing MXFP4 quantization error for LLM reinforcement learning: reducible bias, recoverable deadzone, and an irreducible floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20402)
*Xiaocan Li,Shiliang Wu,Zheng Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对MXFP4算术在LLM强化学习后训练中的量化误差进行了精确的三路分解，将其分为尺度偏差、死区截断和网格噪声三个分量，并针对每个分量设计了相应的纠错方法，在Qwen2.5-3B和Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base模型上将精度恢复至BF16的0.7%和3.0%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将MXFP4量化误差视为整体噪声，忽略了各误差分量对强化学习训练的不同影响机制，导致精度严重下降。

Method: 1) 将MXFP4量化误差精确分解为尺度偏差（幂次舍入）、死区截断（小值归零）和网格噪声（4比特网格舍入）三个加性分量；2) 分析每个分量主导的RL训练失效模式；3) 提出针对性纠错方法：宏块缩放（降尺度偏差）、异常值回退（恢复死区条目）、自适应量化噪声AQN（控制策略熵）。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-3B密集模型上恢复BF16精度的0.7%以内，在Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base MoE模型上恢复至3.0%以内。

Conclusion: 通过分解量化误差并针对不同RL失效模式设计纠错方法，可有效缓解MXFP4在LLM强化学习后训练中的精度损失。

Abstract: MXFP4 arithmetic can dramatically accelerate reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs), yet the quantization error introduces severe accuracy degradation. Existing work treats the quantization error as a monolithic noise term, missing the distinct mechanisms upon interpreting how quantization error damages training. We prove an exact three-way decomposition of quantization error and show how each component dominates a distinct RL training pathway. Our theoretical and empirical analysis decomposes the MXFP4 quantization error into three additive components: "scale bias" from power-of-two rounding, "deadzone truncation" from zeroing small values, and "grid noise" from rounding to the nearest 4-bit grid. Each component dominates a distinct RL failure mode: scale bias accumulates multiplicatively through the backward pass, affecting gradient accuracy; deadzone truncation degrades rollout quality; and grid noise raises the policy's entropy. We combine corrections that are RL failure mode-targeted but not component-exclusive: Macro-block scaling to reduce scale bias, Outlier Fallback recovers deadzone entries, but also partially reduces scale bias induced error, and Adaptive Quantization Noise (AQN) for controlling the policy entropy. On Qwen2.5-3B dense and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base mixture-of-experts model, the targeted corrections recover BF16 accuracy to within 0.7% and 3.0% respectively.

</details>


### [133] [Spectral Souping: A Unified Framework for Online Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20408)
*Yinlam Chow,Guy Tennenholtz,Ted Yun,James Harrison,Arthur Gretton,Andre Barreto,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Spectral Souping框架，通过发现LLM中的通用谱表示实现高效模型合并，先离线学习多个专业化策略，再在线融合以适应个体用户偏好，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF虽能对齐LLM与整体人类偏好，但无法处理个体用户多样且冲突的需求，现有方法缺乏高效、可扩展的在线个性化对齐方案。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) 离线阶段学习一组基策略，每个策略专注于一个细粒度偏好维度；(2) 在线适应阶段通过合并策略的输出或参数（"souping"）实现推理时快速调整，无需昂贵的在线重训练。

Result: 在在线偏好对齐基准测试中，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法，展示了可扩展且计算高效的性能提升。

Conclusion: Spectral Souping为动态适应个体用户偏好提供了一种可扩展、计算高效的解决方案，核心发现是LLM中存在高度适合模型合并的通用谱表示。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) effectively aligns Large Language Models (LLMs) with aggregate human preferences but often fails to address the diverse and conflicting needs of individual users. To overcome this issue, we introduce Spectral Souping, a unified framework for efficient, online preference alignment. Our contribution is the discovery of a universal spectral representation within LLMs, which is proven to be highly amenable to model merging. This theoretical insight enables a two-phase methodology: we first learn a basis of specialized policies offline, each focused on a distinct, fine-grained preference dimension. An online adaptation algorithm then efficiently ``soups'' these policies at inference time, either by merging their outputs or parameters, enabling rapid model adaptation without the need for costly online retraining w.r.t. tailored preference rewards. Experiments on online preference alignment benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves significant performance improvements over existing state-of-the-art approaches, presenting a scalable and computationally efficient solution for dynamically adapting LLMs to individual user preferences.

</details>


### [134] [Supervised Latent Restructuring for Small-Data Quantum Learning in Plant Phenomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20413)
*Alakananda Mitra,David H. Fleisher,Vangimalla Reddy,Chittaranjan Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高维小样本生物数据场景下的分类问题，提出了一种结合PCA压缩、LDA监督重构和GPU加速量子核对齐（QKA）的混合工作流。实验表明监督潜空间重构显著提升几何可分性（Silhouette系数从-0.006提升至0.197），但经典分类器表现存在权衡，且QKA在该压缩区间仍面临挑战，揭示了潜空间几何在小样本量子学习中的关键作用及实际难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维生物数据存在特征维度与样本量严重不匹配的问题，在极小样本场景下可靠分类极为困难。核方法在潜压缩未能保留类区分结构时会丧失判别力，亟需探索适用于小样本量子学习的有效表示几何策略。

Method: 提出混合工作流：将1280维深度图像嵌入压缩至64维PCA空间，再通过LDA重构为11维监督潜空间，随后在NVIDIA L40S硬件上进行GPU加速的量子核对齐（QKA）。

Result: 监督潜空间重构显著提升Silhouette系数（从PCA-64的-0.006到LDA-11的0.197）。经典分类器表现分化：线性SVM和XGBoost提升，RBF-SVM和随机森林下降。在受限优化预算下，QKA在该压缩区间仍表现挑战性。

Conclusion: 潜空间几何是小样本量子学习中关键的设计变量，但仅靠几何可分性不足以支撑强可训练量子性能，从过度压缩的生物表示中恢复非线性判别结构在实践中仍存在困难。

Abstract: High-dimensional biological data often exhibit a severe mismatch between feature dimensionality and sample size, making reliable classification difficult in extremely small-data regimes. In these settings, kernel methods can lose discriminative power when latent compression fails to preserve class-separating structure. We study this problem in fine-grained plant phenomics and propose a hybrid workflow that compresses 1280-dimensional deep image embeddings into a 64-dimensional PCA space and then restructures them into an 11-dimensional supervised latent space using Linear Discriminant Analysis (LDA), followed by GPU-accelerated Quantum Kernel Alignment (QKA) on NVIDIA L40S hardware. Empirically, supervised latent restructuring substantially improves the geometric separability of the compressed representation, increasing the Silhouette coefficient from 0.003 in the raw embedding space and -0.006 in PCA-64 to 0.197 in the supervised LDA-11 space. However, downstream classical evaluation reveals a clear compression trade-off: Linear SVM and XGBoost improve in the restructured latent space, whereas RBF-SVM and Random Forest degrade under the same 11-dimensional bottleneck. Under a constrained optimization budget, QKA in this regime remains challenging, indicating that latent geometry alone is not sufficient for strong trainable quantum performance. These findings position representation geometry as a central design variable in small-data quantum learning and expose the practical difficulty of recovering nonlinear discriminative structure from aggressively compressed biological representations.

</details>


### [135] [Miller-Index-Based Latent Crystallographic Fracture Plane Reasoning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20416)
*Qinwu Xu,Yifan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能否将晶面指数（米勒指数）作为结构化潜在表征来推理断裂几何。通过大量实验，作者发现MLLMs在理想化条件下能可靠地进行潜在推理，并能在物理不支持时拒绝该潜在表征，表明MLLMs可充当物理感知推理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索MLLMs是否能够利用米勒指数这一结构化潜在表征来推理断裂几何，验证其在物理感知推理方面的能力。

Method: 将米勒指数$z=(h,k,l)$形式化为控制理想化平面断裂的潜在变量，评估两个互补能力：(i) 潜在推理——模型将视觉观测映射到物理有效条件下的平面假设；(ii) 潜在适用性评估——模型判断该表征对给定断裂图像是否有意义。实验涵盖合成数据、受控2D-3D几何对以及多种材料（陶瓷、玻璃、金属、混凝土）的真实断裂图像。

Result: MLLMs在理想化设置中能可靠执行潜在推理，并且在底层物理不支持时能够拒绝使用该潜在表征。

Conclusion: 只要显式建模有效性域，MLLMs可以作为基于结构化潜在先验的物理感知推理系统。

Abstract: We study whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can leverage crystallographic plane indices (Miller indices) as a structured latent representation for reasoning about fracture geometry. We formulate Miller indices $z = (h,k,l)$ as a latent variable governing idealized planar fracture and evaluate two complementary capabilities: (i) latent inference, where the model maps visual observations to plane hypotheses under physically valid conditions, and (ii) latent applicability assessment, where the model determines whether such a representation is meaningful for a given fracture image.
  Through extensive experiments spanning synthetic data, controlled 2D--3D geometric pairs, and real-world fracture images across multiple material classes -- including ceramics, glass, metals, and concrete -- we show that MLLMs can reliably perform latent inference in idealized settings and, critically, can reject the latent representation when the underlying physics does not support it. These results suggest that MLLMs can act as physics-aware reasoning systems conditioned on structured latent priors, provided that the domain of validity is explicitly modeled.

</details>


### [136] [Can Conversational XAI Improve User Performance? An Experimental Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20439)
*Sven Kruschel,Julian Rosenberger,Lasse Bohlen,Mathias Kraus,Patrick Zschech*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个评估对话式XAI辅助的实验设计，通过可解释模型让用户识别并补偿系统误差。初步实验(N=42)显示两种辅助类型均显著提升用户表现，但两者间无显著差异，且整体参与度不高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI技术虽旨在提升用户表现，但往往效果有限。对话式XAI助手承诺克服这些局限，但缺乏关于其对客观性能指标影响的实证证据。

Method: 采用可解释性设计的预测模型，创建用户可通过识别和补偿系统错误来超越模型的条件；比较对话式辅助与基于问答的辅助；通过预测准确率、模型理解和错误识别三个维度评估。

Result: 初步实验(N=42)显示两组参与者的表现均显著优于模型，但辅助类型间无显著性能差异，且整体参与度不高。

Conclusion: 研究发现为后续完整研究提供了改进方向，包括增强参与度的干预措施以及探究改善预测的机制。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) techniques aim to provide insights into predictive models and enhance user performance, yet they often fall short of these expectations. Conversational XAI assistants promise to overcome such limitations, but empirical evidence on their impact on objective performance measures remains limited. We propose an experimental design for evaluating explanation assistance through prediction accuracy, model understanding, and error identification. Using an explainable-by-design prediction model, we create conditions where users can outperform the model by identifying and compensating for systematic errors. We compare conversational assistance against Q&A-based assistance to assess which better supports users in working with model explanations. Preliminary results from testing our experimental design show that participants (N=42) in both treatments significantly outperformed the model but reveal no performance differences between assistance types and modest engagement overall. These findings inform refinements for our planned full study, including enhanced engagement interventions and investigation of the mechanisms driving improved predictions.

</details>


### [137] [Weight Decay Regimes in Grokking Transformers: Cheap Online Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20441)
*Lucky Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在模算术任务上训练的Transformer模型，发现权重衰减（weight decay）是控制模型在记忆、泛化和崩溃之间相变的关键标量参数。研究提出了两种低成本在线诊断指标——平均成对注意力头余弦相似度和熵标准差，仅从注意力激活中跟踪训练动态。实验覆盖11种条件、3种模型规模（0.82M–85M参数），定位了记忆-发展（developmental）边界λ_c=0.0158，并给出了经验幂律指数ν=0.757。多任务、注意力头重初始化及跨架构（MLP、LSTM、Mamba）实验均验证了权重衰减控制模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer在模算术任务上从记忆到泛化再到崩溃的相变机制，寻找低成本的在线诊断方法，以及验证权重衰减作为控制参数的有效性。

Method: 采用权重衰减作为标量控制参数，提出平均成对注意力头余弦相似度和熵标准差两个在线诊断指标。在11种实验条件下进行系统实验，包括不同模型规模、多任务复制、注意力头重初始化、权重范数裁剪对比，以及跨架构（MLP、LSTM、Mamba）探测。使用逻辑斯蒂拟合定位相变边界，幂律拟合估计经验指数。

Result: 确定了记忆-发展边界λ_c=0.0158（95% CI [0.0109, 0.0200]），经验幂律指数ν=0.757（CI [0.725, 0.799]），该指数与参考值ν=1/2和3D Ising ν≈0.63均不重合。注意力头重初始化实验显著改变Phase-2振幅（Cohen's d=-1.190, p=4.5×10^-3），而权重范数裁剪无此效果。跨架构实验均复现了权重衰减控制的相变模式，但λ_c值因架构而异。

Conclusion: 权重衰减是模算术任务中Transformer模型相变的经验控制参数；提出的两种在线诊断指标有效且计算成本低；经验幂律指数ν=0.757表明该相变可能与已知普适类不同，但需更密集的有限尺寸标度研究确认；主要结论限于小模型注意机制下的模算术任务。

Abstract: Transformers trained on modular arithmetic exhibit sharp transitions between memorization, generalization, and collapse. We show that weight decay acts as a scalar empirical control parameter for these regimes, and introduce two cheap online diagnostics, mean pairwise attention-head cosine similarity and entropy standard deviation, that track training dynamics from attention activations alone and complement loss-landscape diagnostics at lower compute cost. Across eleven experimental conditions and three model scales (0.82M to 85M parameters), the weight-decay axis separates memorization, developmental grokking, and collapse. A near-transition logistic fit localizes the memorization-to-developmental boundary at $λ_c=0.0158$ (95% CI [0.0109, 0.0200], N=210); a power-law fit gives an empirical exponent $ν=0.757$ (CI [0.725, 0.799]). Reference exponents $ν=1/2$ and 3D Ising $ν\approx 0.63$ lie outside this empirical CI under our four-bin grid, so we report $ν$ as empirical and defer universality-class identification to denser finite-size-scaling work. A horizon-matched multi-task replication (n=280, four modular operations) preserves the weight-decay control pattern; a paired attention-head re-initialization experiment at $λ=0.05$ changes Phase-2 amplitude (Cohen's $d=-1.190$, n=10, $p_t=4.5 \times 10^{-3}$), while matched weight-norm clipping does not. Three cross-architecture probes (4L MLP, 4L LSTM, and 4L Mamba; each n=70) replicate the weight-decay-controlled transition with architecture-specific $λ_c$ values. Main diagnostic claims are scoped to modular arithmetic in small transformer attention models; the non-attention experiments are scope probes, and architecture-wide, language-model, and universality-class claims are out of scope.

</details>


### [138] [LLM Pretraining Shapes a Generalizable Manifold: Insights into Cross-Modal Transfer to Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20449)
*Alexis Roger,Prateek Humane,Zhenghan Tai,Gwen Legate,Andrei Mircea,Vasilii Feofanov,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明语言预训练Transformer能有效进行时间序列预测，原因是预训练构建了可复用的流形结构，微调仅需低维对齐而非从头学习时间基元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言预训练Transformer为何能有效进行时间序列预测，并揭示跨模态迁移的内在机制。

Method: 通过线性探针、检索实验、优化动态分析、低秩更新、子空间对齐及特征共享分析等方法，从几何角度解释LLM到时间序列的迁移机制。

Result: 冻结LLM状态的线性探针可无需配对监督解码时间序列轨迹；预训练初始化产生相干梯度和高度各向异性的损失景观；微调表现为低维对齐，复用已有方向而非从头学习。

Conclusion: 语言预训练构建了可复用的流形，微调将数值动态投影到任务相关方向上，从而支持跨模态迁移。

Abstract: Can language-pretrained transformers become effective time-series forecasters, and why? In this paper, we show that cross-modal transfer arises because language pretraining preconditions time series training with a reusable manifold. A linear probe on frozen LLM states decodes realistic time-series trajectories without paired supervision, and retrieval in this projected space yields competitive forecasts, showing that structure and dynamics exist before finetuning. Pretrained initialization also improves optimization, producing coherent gradients and a highly anisotropic loss landscape unlike random initialization. Finetuning then acts as low-dimensional alignment, reusing existing directions rather than learning temporal primitives from scratch, as evidenced by low-rank updates, subspace alignment, and shared features for periodicity, trend, and repetition. Together, these results support a geometric account of LLM-to-time-series transfer: language pretraining builds the manifold, and finetuning projects numerical dynamics onto task-relevant directions.

</details>


### [139] [CASCADE Conformal Prediction: Uncertainty-Adaptive Prediction Intervals for Two-Stage Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20468)
*Ricardo Diaz-Rincon,Muxuan Liang,Adolfo Ramirez-Zamora,Benjamin Shickel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CASCADE框架，通过将分类任务中的认知不确定性传播到回归任务中，动态调整帕金森病患者左旋多巴等效日剂量（LEDD）预测的置信区间，在自信病例上区间窄38.9%，同时保证不确定病例的覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病用药管理因疾病进展异质性、患者反应差异及副作用而困难；现有AI模型虽能预测LEDD，但标准不确定性量化无法区分高置信度与低置信度预测，导致临床决策中高风险与低风险预测被等同对待。

Method: 提出CASCADE（Calibrated Adaptive Scaling via Conformal And Distributional Estimation）框架，利用Venn-Abers多概率不确定性将分类任务（是否需要调整用药）的认知不确定性映射为非一致性分数，动态缩放回归任务（调整多少）的预测区间，实现从分类到回归的级联效应。

Result: 对于高置信度患者，预测区间较标准共形方法窄38.9%；对于不确定病例，区间自动扩展以保持鲁棒覆盖。

Conclusion: CASCADE通过级联不确定性传播，弥合了离散临床决策与连续剂量预测之间的差距，为帕金森病个性化用药管理提供了更可靠的不确定性量化工具。

Abstract: Effective medication management in Parkinson's Disease (PD) is challenging due to heterogeneous disease progression, variable patient response, and medication side effects. While AI models can forecast levodopa equivalent daily dose (LEDD) as a measure of medication needs, standard uncertainty quantification often fails to communicate the reliability of these predictions, treating high and low confidence clinical decisions identically. We introduce CASCADE (Calibrated Adaptive Scaling via Conformal And Distributional Estimation), a novel conformal prediction framework that propagates epistemic uncertainty from a screening classifier to adapt downstream predictions. Unlike standard conformal methods that rely on auxiliary residual regression, we leverage epistemic uncertainty from a primary classification task (identifying whether a medication change is needed) to dynamically scale the prediction intervals of a secondary regression task (predicting how much change). By mapping Venn-Abers multi-probabilistic uncertainty directly to non-conformity scores, our framework achieves continuous risk adaptation. We demonstrate that this ``cascade effect'' produces highly efficient intervals for confident patients (38.9% narrower than standard conformal baselines) while automatically expanding intervals to ensure robust coverage for uncertain cases, bridging the gap between discrete clinical decision-making and continuous dose forecasting in PD.

</details>


### [140] [ZEBRA: Zero-shot Budgeted Resource Allocation for LLM Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20485)
*May Hamri,Inbal Talgam-Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZEBRA是一种零样本框架，将多智能体管线的预算分配问题简化为连续非线性背包问题，通过LLM估计每阶段效用曲线并使用注水搜索求解拉格朗日乘子，在APPS编码基准和HotpotQA问答管线上显著优于LLM直接分配预算的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预算感知方法要么在单智能体内逐步控制推理过程，要么通过强化学习学习资源分配策略，但都无法解决多智能体管线在推理时如何在各个组成阶段之间分配预算的问题。

Method: ZEBRA将多阶段预算分配建模为连续非线性背包问题：LLM控制器估计每阶段的效用曲线，然后通过注水搜索（water-filling search）求解拉格朗日乘子，得到每阶段的预算分配方案。加性和乘性聚合方式在同一个求解器下统一处理。

Result: 在APPS 150个任务的编码基准测试中，ZEBRA变体在所有聚合指标上均优于LLM直接分配预算。在预算为无约束支出的α=0.5时，ZEBRA恢复了94.4%的无约束质量，而LLM-direct仅为88.1%。在HotpotQA三阶段管线上，ZEBRA比LLM-direct高出14.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 轻量级的算法引导推理时间预算分配能改善自主多智能体系统的经济行为，且ZEBRA的分配策略能根据管线结构自适应调整（APPS偏向精炼阶段，HotpotQA接近平衡分配）。

Abstract: As autonomous agents increasingly execute end-to-end tasks under fixed monetary budgets, the pressing open question shifts from whether the budget is respected, to how to spend it effectively. Existing budget-aware methods typically control reasoning step-by-step within a single agent, or learn resource allocation policies via RL. None address how to split a budget across the composing phases of a multi-agent pipeline at inference time. We propose ZEBRA, a zero-shot framework that reduces multi-phase budget allocation to a continuous nonlinear knapsack problem: an LLM controller estimates per-phase utility curves, and a water-filling search on the Lagrange multiplier returns the per-phase split. Additive and multiplicative aggregations are unified under the same solver. On a $150$-task APPS coding benchmark, both ZEBRA variants outperform LLM-direct (budget allocation directly by an LLM) on every aggregate metric. At a budget of $α= 0.5$ of the unconstrained spend, ZEBRA recovers $94.4\%$ of unconstrained quality, versus $88.1\%$ for LLM-direct. The advantage is statistically significant and transfers beyond coding: on a $3$-phase HotpotQA pipeline, ZEBRA beats LLM-direct by $14.3$pp, with allocations empirically robust to curve-estimation noise. On HotpotQA, ZEBRA arrives at a different budget split (near-balanced) compared to the APPS one (skewed towards a refinement phase), showing adaptation to the pipeline structure. More broadly, we show that lightweight algorithmic guidance at inference time can improve the economic behavior of autonomous multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [141] [Tippett-minimum Fusion of Representation-space Diffusion Models for Multi-Encoder Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20502)
*Neelkamal Bhuyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EncMin2L方法，通过多编码器融合表示空间扩散模型（RDM），利用两类无OOD标签的诊断指标（η²和Δμ）识别编码器对不同分布偏移的敏感性，并通过两层min门控机制组合各编码器的似然检测结果，在参数减少2.3倍的情况下，同时在四种分布偏移类型（全局域变化、语义差异、纹理差异、协变量污染）上实现≥0.94 AUROC，超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法通常针对单一类型的分布偏移设计，缺乏能够同时处理全局域变化、语义差异、纹理差异和协变量污染等多种分布偏移类型的统一框架。此外，多编码器集成方法参数开销大，且难以在不使用OOD标签的情况下有效组合各编码器的检测信号。

Method: 提出EncMin2L框架：(1) 对每个编码器训练独立的表示空间扩散模型(RDM)；(2) 通过两类无OOD标签的诊断指标（η²：基于类条件F检验；Δμ：合成污染下的对数似然偏移）量化各编码器对不同偏移类型的敏感性；(3) 采用两层min门控机制（Tippett最小p值组合）聚合各编码器的扩散似然检测结果，实现无OOD标签的校准与组合。整体架构为编码器无关的两级min门控。

Result: EncMin2L在全局域变化、语义差异、纹理差异和协变量污染四种偏移类型上同时达到≥0.94 AUROC，参数开销比单编码器基线降低2.3倍，在所有重叠基准测试中均超越现有的表示空间扩散OOD检测器。

Conclusion: 通过多编码器融合和编码器特定的扩散模型，结合无监督的诊断指标和两级min门控组合策略，能够高效、鲁棒地处理全谱系分布偏移的OOD检测问题，验证了编码器特异性与多源信息融合在OOD检测中的有效性。

Abstract: We address out-of-distribution (OOD) detection across the full spectrum of distribution shifts -- global domain changes, semantic divergence, texture differences, and covariate corruptions -- through a multi-encoder fusion of per-encoder representation-space diffusion models (RDMs). We statistically identify each encoder's sensitivity to specific shift types from ID data alone and introduce EncMin2L -- an encoder-agnostic two-level $\min(\cdot)$-gate that combines and calibrates per-encoder diffusion-based likelihood detectors without OOD labels, outperforming monolithic multi-encoder baselines at $2.3\times$ lower parameter cost. Two ID-data diagnostics: $η^2$ (class-conditional F-test) and $Δμ$ (log-likelihood shift under synthetic corruptions) -- quantify encoder specialization, while a Tippett minimum $p$-value combination aggregates per-encoder scores into a single, calibration-stable OOD signal. EncMin2L achieves $\geq 0.94$ AUROC across all four shift types simultaneously, outperforming the state-of-the-art representation-space diffusion OOD detectors across overlapping benchmarks.

</details>


### [142] [Reinforcing Human Behavior Simulation via Verbal Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20506)
*Weiwei Sun,Xuhui Zhou,Jiarui Liu,Weihua Du,Haojia Sun,Yiqing Xie,Qianou Ma,Sihao Chen,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sherry Wu,Sean Welleck,Graham Neubig,Yiming Yang,Maarten Sap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DITTO框架，将自然语言反馈作为强化学习的一级信号，结合GRPO算法优化LLM，使其能模拟人类行为；同时构建SOUL基准测试集，涵盖10项任务，DITTO相比基础模型平均提升36%，在6/10任务上超越GPT-5.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM从反馈中学习主要集中在代码和数学等可用标量奖励直接验证的领域，但LLM用于模拟人类行为（如用户、患者、学生等角色）时，需要处理的是**口头、主观、多维度**的反馈信号，现有方法无法有效利用这类信号。

Method: 提出**DITTO**框架：每次 rollout 后接收口头反馈，生成反馈条件下的改进 rollout；联合使用**GRPO**对两者进行优化，将口头指引蒸馏到基础策略中，测试时无需反馈。同时构建**SOUL**基准，涵盖10个任务（心理理论、角色扮演、社交技能、学习者模拟、用户模拟、人物模拟）。

Result: DITTO相比基础模型平均提升36%，在SOUL的10个基准中6个超过GPT-5.4，证明基于口头反馈的RL是训练LLM模拟人类行为的有效方向。

Conclusion: 口头反馈作为强化学习的一级信号是训练LLM模拟人类行为的有效方向，DITTO框架验证了其可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Humans learn social norms and behaviors from verbal feedback (e.g., a parent saying "that was rude" or a friend explaining "here's why that hurt"). Yet, learning from feedback for LLMs has largely focused on domains like code and math, where RL rewards are directly verifiable and condensed into scalar values. As LLMs are increasingly used to simulate human behavior, e.g., standing in for users, patients, students, and other personas, there is a pressing need to make them more human-like, which requires embracing a fundamentally different kind of signal: feedback that is verbal, subjective, and multi-faceted. We present DITTO, a model trained by treating verbal feedback as a first-class signal in reinforcement learning. After each rollout, DITTO receives verbal feedback and generates a feedback-conditioned improved rollout; both outputs are jointly optimized with GRPO, distilling verbal guidance into the base policy without requiring feedback at test time. We also introduce SOUL (Simulation gym Of hUman-Like behavior), a unified benchmark and training data suite spanning 10 tasks across six categories: Theory of Mind, character role play, social skill, learner simulation, user simulation, and persona simulation. DITTO achieves an average 36% improvement over the base model and exceeds GPT-5.4 on 6 of 10 SOUL benchmarks, demonstrating that RL with verbal feedback is a promising direction for training LLMs to simulate human behavior.

</details>


### [143] [Online Conformal Prediction with Corrupted Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20515)
*Bowen Wang,Matteo Zecchin,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线共形预测（OCP）在反馈被噪声、通信故障或对抗性篡改（即腐败反馈）场景下的稳健性问题，提出两种鲁棒方案：基于滤波的鲁棒OCP和基于主动补偿的鲁棒OCP，并给出严格的误覆盖理论保证，实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCP在序列和非平稳环境中提供校准的不确定性估计，但其保证依赖于完美覆盖反馈的假设。实际中反馈常常被噪声、通信故障或对抗性攻击破坏，导致校准性能严重下降，因此需要研究腐败反馈下OCP的鲁棒方法。

Method: 1. 将反馈腐败建模为任意二元翻转序列；2. 提出鲁棒OCP via filtering，利用预测阈值的结构性质过滤腐败反馈；3. 提出鲁棒OCP via active compensation，加入主动补偿机制缓解腐败反馈影响；4. 对独立随机翻转模型和有记忆边界的任意错误模型分别给出专门的误覆盖保证。

Result: 在真实数据集上，提出的两种鲁棒方法相比基线OCP方法，在校准性能上显著提升，且预测集大小更小。

Conclusion: 腐败反馈会严重破坏OCP的校准保证，而本文提出的滤波和主动补偿两种鲁棒方案能够有效恢复校准性能，并为在线不确定性估计在现实部署中的可靠性提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Modern artificial intelligence systems require calibrated uncertainty estimates that remain reliable in sequential and non-stationary environments. Online conformal prediction (OCP) addresses this challenge through adaptively updated prediction sets that provide deterministic long-run miscoverage guarantees. These guarantees, however, hinge on the assumption of perfect feedback about the coverage of past prediction sets. In practice, the observed miscoverage indicator may be corrupted by noise, communication failures, or adversarial manipulation, which can severely degrade OCP's calibration guarantees. In this paper, we study OCP under corrupted feedback. We first model feedback corruption as an arbitrary binary flip sequence, and analyze how feedback corruption affects and degrades the miscoverage performance of standard OCP. We then propose two robust schemes: robust OCP via filtering, which leverages the structural properties of the predicted threshold to filter corrupted feedback, and robust OCP via active compensation, which incorporates an active compensation mechanism to mitigate the effect of corrupted feedback. For both methods, we establish explicit miscoverage guarantees, which are further specialized for an independent stochastic flip model and for an arbitrary error model with memory bounds. Experiments on real-world datasets validate the proposed approach, showing markedly improved calibration and significantly smaller prediction sets compared with baseline OCP methods under corrupted feedback.

</details>


### [144] [An exponential mechanism based on quadratic approximations for fine-tuning machine learning models with privacy guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20521)
*Hoang Tran,Jorge Ramirez,Jiayi Wang,Alberto Bocchinfuso,Christopher Stanley,M. Paul Laiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指数机制的差分隐私微调算法，通过构造结合预训练模型局部二次近似和新数据信息的简单效用函数，实现了封闭形式的多元正态分布精确采样，并引入随机投影策略以适配高维模型。在MNIST和MIMIC数据集上的实验表明，该方法在隐私保护下性能与现有技术相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调预训练模型到小型敏感数据集时，模型容易记忆个体数据点，导致恶意对手能够提取敏感信息。现有方法缺乏同时保证隐私保护、计算可行性和理论保障的高效微调方案。

Method: 基于指数机制构建随机化算法：设计一个结合预训练模型局部二次近似（二阶泰勒展开）与新数据集信息的简单效用函数，使得指数机制可精确采样自封闭形式的多元正态分布；进一步引入随机投影策略降低高维参数空间的计算复杂度。

Result: 在MNIST基准和MIMIC临床数据集上，该方法与现有差分隐私微调技术相比取得了具有竞争力的性能表现。同时提供了严格的隐私保证、灵敏度边界和精度估计的理论分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的指数机制微调方法能够在保护差分隐私的前提下实现高效的模型微调，其封闭形式的采样避免了繁琐的近似推断，随机投影策略使其可扩展至大规模模型，为隐私保护下的迁移学习提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Fine-tuning adapts a pretrained machine learning model to a small, sensitive dataset, but this process risks memorizing individual new data points, making the model vulnerable to adversaries who seek to extract sensitive information. In this work, we develop a randomized algorithm based on the exponential mechanism for fine-tuning while ensuring differential privacy. Our key idea is to construct a simple utility function that combines a local quadratic approximation of the pretrained model with information from the new dataset. The resulting exponential mechanism admits exact sampling from a multivariate normal distribution in closed form. We establish theoretical privacy guarantees, sensitivity bounds, and accuracy estimations for our method. We further introduce a random-projection strategy that makes the approach scalable to high-dimensional models. Numerical experiments on the MNIST benchmark and the MIMIC clinical dataset demonstrate competitive performance against existing differentially private fine-tuning techniques.

</details>


### [145] [Ada2MS: A Hybrid Optimization Algorithm Based on Exponential Mixing of Elementwise and Global Second-Moment Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20533)
*Meng Zhu,Quan Xiao,Weidong Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ada2MS优化算法，通过在逐元素二阶矩估计和全局二阶矩估计之间进行连续指数插值，实现了AdamW行为与动量SGD行为之间的平滑过渡，在视觉任务上取得了有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化算法中，AdamW虽更新稳定、鲁棒性强但泛化性能有时弱于动量方法；动量SGD经仔细调参后泛化更好，但对梯度缩放变化和超参数设置更敏感。为平衡两种范式的优缺点，需要一种能够平滑过渡的优化算法。

Method: 通过连续指数插值在逐元素二阶矩估计与全局二阶矩估计之间进行平滑过渡，实现AdamW类行为与动量SGD类行为之间的无缝切换。

Result: 在视觉任务上，采用统一的优化器比较协议，Ada2MS获得了具有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: Ada2MS成功融合了两种优化范式的优势，提供了一种平衡稳定性和泛化性能的优化方案。

Abstract: Optimization algorithms are core methods by which machine learning models iteratively minimize loss functions, update parameters, learn from data, and improve performance. Momentum SGD and AdamW represent two important optimization paradigms. AdamW produces stable updates and usually has strong robustness across training scenarios, but its generalization performance is sometimes weaker than that of momentum methods. Momentum SGD can often obtain better generalization after careful tuning, but it is more sensitive to gradient-scale variation and hyperparameter settings. To balance the strengths and weaknesses of the two paradigms, this paper proposes Ada2MS, an optimization algorithm that achieves a smooth transition between AdamW-like behavior and momentum-SGD-like behavior through continuous exponential interpolation between elementwise second-moment estimates and global second-moment estimates. On the visual tasks evaluated in this study, Ada2MS obtains competitive results under a unified optimizer-comparison protocol. The code will be released at https://github.com/mengzhu0308/Ada2MS

</details>


### [146] [OpenSeisML: Open Large-Scale Real Seismic and well-log Dataset for Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20539)
*Ipsita Bhar,Huseyin Tuna Erdinc,Thales Souza,Charles Jones,Felix J. Herrmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenSeesML，一个从英国国家数据存储库中公开地震数据构建的真实地震数据集集合，并开发了自动化数据整理管道，旨在支持生成式AI用于地震反演工作流，通过训练生成模型捕获地下属性的统计分布，实现不确定性量化的多个统计一致实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法用于地震反演时，受限于高质量速度模型数据的稀缺性（多数数据由石油天然气公司私有），因此需要构建公开可用的真实地震数据集来填补这一空白。

Method: 从英国国家数据存储库（NDR）中筛选公开地震数据，利用检波器（checkshot）数据建立时间-深度关系，通过插值构建速度模型，实现叠后地震数据的时间-深度转换；开发自动化数据整理管道确保可重复性。

Result: 构建了OpenSeisML数据集集合，并实现了自动化数据整理管道，可用于训练生成模型以捕获地下属性统计分布，进而生成多个统计一致的地下模型实现以支持不确定性量化。

Conclusion: OpenSeisML数据集及自动化管道能够支持生成式AI在地震反演中的应用，通过统计一致的多个实现作为先验信息，有效进行不确定性量化。

Abstract: The advent of machine learning (ML) and computer vision has significantly accelerated seismic inversion workflows by reducing the computational cost of traditionally expensive iterative methods. However, the development and evaluation of ML methods remain limited by the scarcity of realistic velocity models, as most high-quality data are privately owned by oil and gas companies. To address this gap, we present OpenSeisML, a collection of real seismic datasets designed to support generative AI (Gen-AI) workflows for seismic inversion. The datasets are curated from publicly available surveys in the UK National Data Repository (NDR). When seismic volumes are in the time domain and wells are in depth, a time-to-depth conversion is required. We use checkshot data to establish the time-depth relationship and construct a velocity model through interpolation for accurate conversion of post-stack seismic data. Here, we present an automated data curation pipeline that enables seismic data preparation while ensuring reproducibility. The objective is to train a generative model that captures the statistical distribution of subsurface properties, enabling the synthesis of multiple statistically consistent realizations for uncertainty quantification which can act as a prior for seismic inversion.

</details>


### [147] [Latent Process Generator Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20547)
*Lukas Billera,Hedwig Nora Nordlinder,Ben Murrell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了潜过程生成匹配（Latent Process Generator Matching）框架，将观测生成状态视为可处理马尔可夫过程Y_t的确定性映射X_t=Φ(Y_t)，并证明可学习像空间上具有与投影过程相同单时间边际分布的随机过程生成器，从而将生成匹配从静态潜变量扩展到时变潜条件过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配和扩散生成模型在训练中依赖辅助随机动力学，但辅助状态在生成时要么难以采样，要么不属于期望输出。虽然已有生成匹配理论形式化了静态潜随机变量的条件化，但缺乏处理时变潜过程的统一框架。

Method: 将观测生成状态X_t定义为可处理马尔可夫过程Y_t的确定性映射Φ(Y_t)，在此设定下证明可以学习像空间上的随机过程生成器，使其单时间边际分布与投影过程相同。该框架泛化并包含了文献中的离散潜过程结果，将生成匹配从静态潜变量扩展到时变潜条件过程家族。

Result: 该框架泛化并包含了文献中已有的离散潜过程结果，将生成匹配从静态潜变量扩展到时变潜条件过程家族。

Conclusion: 潜过程生成匹配为处理时变潜条件过程的生成模型提供了统一的理论框架，统一并推广了现有的增广状态投影结果和生成匹配理论。

Abstract: Many recent flow-matching and diffusion-style generative models rely on auxiliary stochastic dynamics during training: a richer process is simulated to define conditional targets, but the auxiliary state is either intractable to sample at generation time or simply not part of the desired output. Existing Generator Matching theory formalises conditioning on static latent random variables, and several recent papers prove special cases of projection results for particular augmented-state constructions. We introduce latent process generator matching, a general framework that treats the observed generative state as a deterministic image $X_t=Φ(Y_t)$ of a tractable Markov process $Y_t$. We show that in this setting one may learn the generator of a stochastic process on the image space which has the same one-time marginal distributions as the projected process. This generalizes and subsumes the discrete latent process results from the literature, and extends Generator Matching from static latent variables to a rich family of time-dependent latent conditional processes.

</details>


### [148] [Deep Learning Surrogates for Emulating Stochastic Climate Tipping Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20580)
*Adeline Hillier,Jennifer Sleeman,Jay Brett,Caroline Tang,Jenelle Millison,Anand Gnanadesikan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了基于动力学的时序融合Transformer（TFT）作为地球系统模拟的数据驱动替代模型，在21个非平稳时间序列上实现数千时间步的预测，以465倍加速高保真预测大西洋和太平洋坍塌事件的时间，并保持对参数和初始条件的可微性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球系统模拟（如海洋环流模拟）计算成本极高，需要开发高效的数据驱动替代模型来加速预测，同时保持对关键 tipping events（坍塌事件）的预测能力。

Method: 基于时序融合Transformer（TFT）架构，对21个非平稳时间序列及静态协变量（自由参数和初始条件）进行建模；对架构和目标函数进行改进，使其能够预测数千时间步后的 tipping events 时间，并捕捉系综预测中 transition timing 的随机不确定性。

Result: 替代模型能够高保真预测大西洋和太平洋坍塌的时间，捕获系综预测中 transition timing 的随机不确定性，并实现465倍的计算加速，同时保持对参数和初始条件的可微性。

Conclusion: 基于动力学的TFT可以作为地球系统模拟的高效替代模型，在显著加速的同时保持预测精度和可微性，有利于后续的参数优化和敏感性分析。

Abstract: This work explores a dynamics-informed Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) as a data-driven surrogate for computationally intensive Earth system simulations. Focusing on multivariate time series describing global ocean transport, we demonstrate the surrogate's ability to forecast tip events across thousands of time steps. The data involve up to 21 non-stationary time series in addition to static covariates describing free parameters and initial conditions. Modifications to the architecture and objective function yield a surrogate that anticipates the timing of Atlantic and Pacific collapses to high fidelity and captures the stochastic uncertainty in transition timing across ensemble predictions. The learned surrogate achieves a 465x computational speedup over the numerical simulator while maintaining differentiability with respect to parameters and initial conditions.

</details>


### [149] [TriForces: Augmenting Atomistic GNNs for Transferable Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20581)
*Ali Ramlaoui,Alexandre Duval,Hannah Bull,Victor Schmidt,Hugues Talbot,Fragkiskos D. Malliaros,Joseph Musielewicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TriForces是一种模型无关的三流框架，通过分离组成和结构信息并结合自监督学习，在少量数据下显著提升机器学习原子间势的迁移能力和预测精度，在OMat24上仅用20K样本便将能量MAE降低57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIPs在使用小规模任务特定数据集进行领域迁移时，表征会丢失可访问的组成和结构信息，导致迁移不一致，限制了在实际场景中基于少量昂贵数据的高效适应。

Method: 提出TriForces，一种模型无关的三流框架，将组成信息和结构信息分离处理，并结合自监督学习以保留可迁移的表征。通过学习到的隐空间支持高效相似结构检索。

Result: 在MatBench和QM9上相较于基线方法性能提升，无需DFT标签。在OMat24上，仅用20K样本时能量MAE降低57%，力MAE在各样本量下均有改善。

Conclusion: TriForces通过分离组成-结构信息与自监督学习解决了MLIPs迁移不一致问题，在低数据场景下实现显著精度提升，并开源了多种架构的预训练模型。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) achieve excellent accuracy when trained on large Density Functional Theory (DFT) data. To be useful in practice, they must often be adapted to target chemistries using small and expensive task-specific datasets. However, MLIPs transfer inconsistently across domains, with representations that often loose accessible composition and structure information. To address this, we present TriForces, a model-agnostic three-stream framework that separates composition and structure information, combined with self-supervised learning to preserve transferable representations. TriForces improves performance on MatBench and QM9 over baselines without needing DFT labels and enables efficient similar structure retrieval through its learned latent space. On OMat24, in limited-data training regime, TriForces reduces energy MAE by 57% at 20K samples only and improves force MAE across sample sizes. We release pretrained TriForces variants across multiple MLIP architectures with code at https://github.com/Ramlaoui/triforces.

</details>


### [150] [ReversedQ: Opportunities for Faster Q-Learning in Episodic Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20592)
*Sofia R. Miskala-Dinc,Aviva Prins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限时域情节马尔可夫决策过程中的无模型Q学习，指出现有模型后验采样方法存在延迟学习问题，并提出三种改进策略（值函数更新顺序、更新频率、值函数初始化），称为ReversedQ。实验表明，与RandomizedQ相比，ReversedQ在BDCL任务中将平均累积奖励从9.53%提升至78.78%，在链式MDP中从21.76%提升至61.81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无模型后验采样Q学习方法（如RandomizedQ）存在延迟学习问题，导致学习效率低下，本文旨在通过改进值函数更新顺序、更新频率和初始化方式，加速学习过程。

Method: 以Wang等人的RandomizedQ为基础，提出三种改进：(i) 改变值函数更新顺序，(ii) 调整更新频率，(iii) 优化值函数初始化。将这些改进整合为ReversedQ算法，并通过多个实证研究分别评估每个改进及其累积效果。

Result: 在Bidirectional Diabolical Combination Lock (BDCL)任务中，ReversedQ将缩放平均累积奖励从9.53%提升至78.78%；在链式MDP中，从21.76%提升至61.81%。

Conclusion: 通过调整值函数更新顺序、更新频率和初始化方式，可以显著提升无模型后采样Q学习的性能，ReversedQ在多个环境中大幅优于RandomizedQ。

Abstract: We study model-free Q-learning in finite-horizon episodic Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) with stationary dynamics across episodes. We identify a central issue in nascent model-free posterior-sampling works: the reliance on delayed learning in order to prove theoretical guarantees. In particular, we identify three opportunities for faster learning - (i) value-function update order, (ii) update frequencies, and (iii) value-function initialization. Using Wang et al.'s RandomizedQ as a basis, we illustrate these changes and their individual (as well as cumulative) impact in multiple empirical studies. We find that our combined modifications, termed ReversedQ, improve scaled mean cumulative reward compared to RandomizedQ, from 9.53% to 78.78% in the Bidirectional Diabolical Combination Lock (BDCL), and from 21.76% to 61.81% in a chain MDP.

</details>


### [151] [Unsupervised clustering and classification of upper limb EMG signals during functional movements: a data-driven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20599)
*L. F. Salazar Álvarez,D. Escobar-Saltarén,M. B. Salazar Sánchez,S. C. Henao-Aguirre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对上肢表面肌电信号（sEMG）的四阶段处理流水线，用于功能性抓取动作的聚类与分类。基于NINAPRO DB4数据集，通过预处理（低通滤波+Hilbert包络）、26维特征提取与降维（最终筛选5个关键特征）、基于马氏距离的层次聚类选出6个代表性手势，以及PyCaret自动模型比较（Extra Trees和ANN表现最优），最终实现了稳定、低延迟的肌电假肢控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肌电信号分类方法在处理多手势、高维度特征时存在计算效率低和泛化能力不足的问题，难以满足假肢实时控制的需求。本文旨在设计一种可扩展、低延迟的流水线，通过特征选择和手势聚类来平衡生物力学多样性与计算效率，从而提升分类模型的稳定性和实用性。

Method: 采用四阶段流水线：(1) 预处理：四阶0.6 Hz低通滤波 + Hilbert包络变换；(2) 特征提取：26个时频域指标，经可视化分析、互信息、PCA和决策树重要性评估后筛选为5个关键特征；(3) 手势选择：基于马氏距离的层次聚类，从52个手势中选出6个代表性动作；(4) 分类评估：200 ms时间窗分割，使用PyCaret自动比较多个模型，最终独立训练验证ET和ANN的性能。

Result: Extra Trees和Artificial Neural Networks在PyCaret自动比较中表现最优；后续独立训练证实ANN具有渐进学习能力，ET则保持稳健一致的结果。200 ms窗口在稳定性和生理合理性上为最优时间分割参数。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了所提流水线的有效性，支持自适应、低延迟的肌电假肢控制策略，为未来实时应用提供了可扩展的技术框架。

Abstract: This study presents a comprehensive approach for the clustering and classification of upper-limb surface electromyography (sEMG) signals during functional reach and grasp movements. The methodology was applied to the NINAPRO DB4 dataset, which provides multichannel EMG recordings of 52 gestures. A four-stage pipeline was designed, including signal preprocessing, fea-ture extraction, gesture selection via hierarchical clustering, and comparative model evaluation. Preprocessing involved a fourth-order low-pass filter (0.6 Hz) and Hilbert envelope transformation, effectively reducing noise and enhancing signal clarity. Feature extraction yielded 26 temporal and frequency-domain met-rics, which were later refined using visual analysis, mutual information, principal component analysis, and decision tree importance scores. A final subset of five key features was selected for classification tasks. Gesture selection was per-formed through hierarchical clustering using Mahalanobis distance, resulting in six representative movements that balanced biomechanical diversity and compu-tational efficiency. A 200 ms window was identified as optimal for temporal seg-mentation based on stability and physiological plausibility. Classifier models were evaluated in two stages. Automated comparison using PyCaret identified Extra Trees (ET) and Artificial Neural Networks (ANN) as top performers. Sub-sequent independent training confirmed their stability and generalization capac-ity, with ANN showing progressive learning and ET maintaining robust, con-sistent results. The findings support the implementation of adaptive, low-latency control strategies for myoelectric prostheses and provide a scalable pipeline for future real-time applications.

</details>


### [152] [Compositional Transduction with Latent Analogies for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20609)
*Junseok Kim,Dohyeong Kim,Mineui Hong,Songhwai Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于类比转导(analogy transduction)的离线目标条件强化学习方法，通过捕捉任务执行中的不变类比表示，实现了对未见上下文和目标组合的组合泛化，在OGBench操作环境中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线GCRL方法依赖时间连续片段的轨迹拼接，限制了跨不同上下文的组合行为学习。本文旨在克服这一局限，实现真正的组合泛化。

Method: 形式化类比转导为通过将任务内生类比与给定上下文组合来合成新计划；提出一种类比表示，该表示捕获最优任务执行下的变化、对上下文变化保持不变且足以实现最优目标达成；引入一种新方法使类比转导超越已见类比-上下文对，推广到未见组合。

Result: 在OGBench操作环境中，该方法显著优于未进行类比转导的先前方法。

Conclusion: 类比转导是一种有效的组合泛化机制，所提出的类比表示框架能够显著提升离线GCRL在未见组合场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Compositional generalization is essential for reaching unseen goals under novel contextual variations in offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), where a generalist goal-reaching agent must be learned from limited data. Most prior approaches pursue this via trajectory stitching over temporally contiguous segments, which limits composing behaviors across varying contexts. To overcome this limitation, we formalize analogy transduction as synthesizing new plans by composing task-endogenous analogies with given contexts and propose a novel analogy representation tailored for it. Grounded in our theory, this analogy representation captures what changes under optimal task execution, remains invariant to contextual variations, and is sufficient for optimal goal reaching. We further contend that generalization to unseen analogy-context pairs is a practical obstacle in analogy transduction, and introduce a new approach for offline GCRL that enables analogy transduction beyond seen pairs to unseen combinations. We empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on OGBench manipulation environments, substantially outperforming prior methods that do not perform analogy transduction. Project page: https://rllab-snu.github.io/projects/CTA/

</details>


### [153] [Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20612)
*Ziye Chen,Hongbin Lin,Xinyue Xu,Jie Li,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Matryoshka概念瓶颈模型（MCBM），通过嵌套层次化概念结构实现单模型内的自适应概念利用，将预期干预成本从线性降低到对数级别O(log K)，并保证性能单调提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBM）存在两难困境：要么使用单一静态概念集导致专家需要穷举标注所有概念，干预成本高昂；要么为不同概念预算训练多个模型，带来巨大的计算和维护开销。亟需一种能在单个模型中实现自适应概念利用的方法。

Method: 受Matryoshka表示学习启发，MCBM将概念按最大相关性和最小冗余度组织成嵌套层次结构，无需重新训练即可在不同概念粒度级别进行推理。该架构使得模型能够在单个统一框架内支持从粗粒度到细粒度的灵活概念使用。

Result: 理论上，MCBM将预期干预成本从线性降至对数级别O(log K)，并保证性能单调提升。实验上，MCBM在匹配独立训练模型性能的同时，实现了动态且高效的专家交互。

Conclusion: MCBM成功解决了CBM在实践部署中的核心瓶颈——测试时干预成本过高问题，通过嵌套层次化概念结构实现了单模型内的自适应概念利用，为可解释深度学习提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have emerged as a prominent paradigm for interpretable deep learning, learning by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts. However, their practical deployment is hindered by the high cost of test-time intervention, as correcting model errors typically requires human experts to manually inspect and verify a large set of predicted concepts. Existing approaches suffer from a fundamental structural limitation: they either adopt a single static concept set, forcing experts to exhaustively annotate concepts and incurring prohibitive intervention costs, or train multiple models tailored to different concept budgets, resulting in substantial computational and maintenance overhead. To address this challenge, we propose the Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Model (MCBM), a unified architecture that enables adaptive concept utilization within a single model. Inspired by Matryoshka Representation Learning, MCBM organizes concepts into a nested hierarchy based on maximum relevance and minimum redundancy, allowing inference at multiple levels of conceptual granularity without retraining. Theoretically, we show that MCBM reduces the expected intervention costs from linear to logarithmic order, $O(\log K)$, while guaranteeing monotonic performance improvement. Empirically, extensive experiments demonstrate that MCBM matches the performance of independently trained models while enabling dynamic and efficient expert interaction.

</details>


### [154] [SURF: Steering the Scalarization Weight to Uniformly Traverse the Pareto Front](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20619)
*Liuyuan Jiang,Chentong Huang,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SURF方法，通过几何分析标量化的路径速度不均匀性，推导出基于弧长累积分布函数逆映射的权重采样规则，实现帕累托前沿的均匀覆盖，并在多目标优化问题中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多目标优化中，均匀采样标量化权重通常会导致帕累托前沿（PF）的非均匀覆盖，这种不匹配现象缺乏理论解释和系统性的解决方案。

Method: 通过几何分析标量化路径，发现权重变化时解沿PF的遍历速度不均匀，该速度诱导出弧长累积分布函数（CDF）；通过逆CDF映射得到产生均匀PF覆盖的权重采样规则。SURF方法：对结构化问题（如双目标bandits）推导闭式CDF和权重采样规则；对一般问题交替进行CDF重构和权重采样。

Result: 理论上证明了SURF在可证明条件下线性收敛至不可避免的有限采样下限。实验上，在bandits、多目标-gymnasium和多目标LLM对齐任务中，SURF比基线方法更高效地实现了均匀PF覆盖。

Conclusion: 通过几何视角解释了标量化权重与PF覆盖不匹配的根本原因，并提出了一种通用的、理论保证下的均匀PF覆盖采样方法SURF。

Abstract: Scalarization is widely used in multi-objective optimization owing to its simplicity and scalability. In many applications, the goal is to generate solutions that represent diverse user preferences, ideally with uniform coverage of the Pareto front (PF). However, uniformly sampling scalarization weights usually induces non-uniform coverage of the PF. We explain this mismatch through a geometric analysis of the scalarization path. As the scalarization weight varies, the corresponding solutions trace the PF with a generally non-uniform traversal speed. This speed induces an arc-length cumulative distribution function (CDF); inverting this CDF map yields a principled rule for selecting weights that produce uniform PF coverage. Building on this insight, we propose SURF (Sampling Uniformly along the PaReto Front). For structured problems, including bi-objective bandits, we derive closed-form expressions for this CDF map and the resulting PF-aware weight sampling rule. For general problems, SURF alternates between CDF reconstruction and weight sampling. Theoretically, we show that under provable conditions, SURF converges linearly to an unavoidable finite-sampling floor. Empirically, experiments on bandits, multi-objective-gymnasium, and multi-objective LLM alignment demonstrate that SURF efficiently achieves more uniform PF coverage than baselines.

</details>


### [155] [Dynamic Shapley Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20620)
*Xuan Yang,Hsi-Wen Chen,Ming-Syan Chen,Jian Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对Shapley值数据估值在动态场景下计算成本高的问题，本文提出D-Shap框架，通过将Shapley值表示为玩家×任务矩阵并利用效用局部性和联盟局部性，实现高效的增量更新，任务更新仅需毫秒级，玩家更新成本降低三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Shapley数据估值方法将计算视为一次性过程，输出聚合分数，无法复用。当任务或训练玩家发生变化时需要完全重新计算，计算成本极高，无法适用于动态环境。

Method: 将Shapley值表示为一个玩家×任务矩阵，把动态估值问题形式化为结构化矩阵维护问题。利用两个关键性质——效用局部性（每个任务依赖少量训练玩家）和联盟局部性（相似任务产生相似估值）——设计D-Shap框架：新任务的估值通过结构感知插值推断，新玩家引起的更新仅限局部矩阵块。此外提出自估值（self-valuation）机制，直接从训练数据构建初始矩阵，无需预定义评估任务。

Result: 在多种模型上的实验表明，D-Shap的任务更新可在毫秒级完成，玩家更新成本降低多达三个数量级，且估值质量与完整重计算相当。

Conclusion: D-Shap通过矩阵视角和局部性假设，有效解决了动态场景下Shapley值数据估值的高计算成本问题，为数据估值在持续学习等动态环境中的应用提供了可行的方案。

Abstract: Shapley-based data valuation provides a principled way to quantify the contribution of training data, but its high computational cost makes it impractical in dynamic settings where tasks and training players evolve. Existing methods treat Shapley computation as a one-shot process and collapse contributions into aggregated scores, preventing reuse and requiring recomputation under any change. We introduce a new perspective that represents Shapley values as a player-by-task matrix and formulates dynamic valuation as a structured matrix maintenance problem. We exploit the fact that each task depends on a small subset of training players and that similar tasks yield similar valuations, leading to utility locality and coalition locality. Based on these insights, we propose D-Shap, a dynamic valuation framework that enables efficient updates by modifying only a small portion of the matrix: new task valuations are inferred via structure-aware interpolation, while updates induced by new players are confined to affected local matrix blocks. To eliminate the need for pre-specified evaluation tasks, we introduce self-valuation, which constructs the initial matrix directly from training data, supported by scalable subset reuse and coverage-aware anchor selection. Experiments across diverse models show that D-Shap performs task updates in milliseconds and reduces the cost of player updates by up to three orders of magnitude, while achieving valuation quality competitive with full recomputation.

</details>


### [156] [Same Target, Different Basins: Hard vs. Soft Labels for Annotator Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20642)
*Mirerfan Gheibi,Gashin Ghazizadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在标注者意见不一致时，使用硬标签传递（包括multipass和随机标签采样SLS）替代软标签训练的方法。实验发现，当每个样本的标注数量较少时，硬标签方法优于软标签训练；当完整标注分布可用时，两者性能相当。SLS与软标签交叉熵优化相同目标，硬标签方法收敛到更平坦的盆地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注者之间的分歧反映了认知不确定性而非简单标签噪声。传统方法要么将投票坍缩为单个硬标签，要么直接训练经验软标签分布，但这些方式可能无法有效处理稀疏标注场景下的不确定性。

Method: 提出了两种硬标签传递方法：multipass（循环遍历所有观测投票，保持数据集大小固定）和随机标签采样SLS（每个epoch开始时为每个样本采样一个标签）。在CIFAR-10H上使用少量标注进行实验，并通过确定性控制（作为multipass的消融）和打乱SLS（打破样本与分布的匹配）作为对照。

Result: 小样本标注下硬标签方法优于软标签训练，且改进幅度随经验分布与完整标注分布差距增大而增大；完整分布下两者相当。SLS与软标签交叉熵优化相同期望目标。硬标签方法收敛到更平坦的盆地，在SVHN和CIFAR-100的OOD检测中提供了支持性证据。

Conclusion: 当原始投票计数可用时，multipass是强默认选择；当每个样本仅有少量投票时，SLS是轻量级竞争性替代方案；当完整标注分布可用时，SLS与软标签训练性能相当。

Abstract: When annotators disagree, that disagreement can reflect epistemic uncertainty rather than simple label noise. We study hard-label delivery as an alternative to the usual choices of collapsing votes to a single label or training directly on the empirical soft-label distribution. We focus on two primary hard-label methods: multipass, which cycles through observed votes while keeping the dataset size fixed, and stochastic label sampling (SLS), which samples one label per example at the start of each epoch. On CIFAR-10H, we find that when only a small number of annotations per example is available, hard-label delivery improves over soft-label training, with larger improvements where the sparse empirical target is farther from the full annotator distribution. When full annotator distributions are available, both hard-label methods match soft-label training. We use deterministic control as an ablation of multipass and shuffled SLS as a control that breaks the example-to-distribution match. We also show that SLS and soft-label cross-entropy optimize the same expected objective. Hard-label delivery also converges to flatter basins, with supporting descriptive evidence from OOD detection on SVHN and CIFAR-100. Overall, these results suggest that multipass is a strong practical default when raw vote counts are available, while SLS offers a lightweight alternative that remains competitive when only a few votes per example are available and matches soft-label training when full annotator distributions are available.

</details>


### [157] [AVSD: Adaptive-View Self-Distillation by Balancing Consensus and Teacher-Specific Privileged Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20643)
*Duy Nguyen,Hanqi Xiao,Archiki Prasad,Zaid Khan,Anirban Das,Austin Zhang,Sambit Sahu,Hyunji Lee,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AVSD（自适应视图自蒸馏）方法，通过从多个特权信息视图中分离稳定跨视图共识与视图特定残差信号，解决了自蒸馏中教师-学生不对称及最优视图选择难题，在数学竞赛和代码生成基准上超越单视图自蒸馏和GRPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏中教师依赖学生无法访问的特权信息造成根本性不对称，且最优特权信息类型往往因任务而异，难以选择单一教师视图。现有方法未能同时解决这两个挑战。

Method: AVSD通过重建令牌级监督，分离跨视图共识信号（提供可靠更新方向）与视图特定残差信号，当残差信号与共识方向一致且幅度成比例时选择性添加以调整更新幅度。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25数学竞赛上，AVSD在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B上平均Avg@8增益分别达3.1%和2.2%；在Codeforces、LiveCodeBench v6代码生成任务上，在Qwen3-8B上平均超单视图基线2.4%。

Conclusion: AVSD通过多视图自适应蒸馏策略，有效解决了视图选择困境和师生不对称问题，在数学推理和代码生成任务上均取得一致提升。

Abstract: Self-distillation enables language models to learn on-policy from their own trajectories by using the same model as both student and teacher, with the teacher being conditioned on privileged information unavailable to the student. Such information can come in different types or views, such as solutions, demonstrations, feedback, or final answers. This setup provides dense token-level feedback without relying on a separate external model, but creates a fundamental asymmetry: the teacher may rely on view-specific information that the student cannot access at inference time. Moreover, the best type of privileged information is often task-dependent, making it difficult to choose a single teacher view. In this work, we address both these challenges jointly by introducing AVSD (Adaptive-View Self-Distillation), a novel method of self-distillation with multiple privileged-information views, which reconstructs token-level supervision by separating stable cross-view consensus from view-specific residual signals. AVSD identifies the consensus signal shared across views, which provides a reliable update direction, and then selectively adds the view-specific residual signal to adjust the update magnitude when it both aligns with the consensus direction and remains proportionate to the consensus signal. Experiments on math competition benchmarks (AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25) show that AVSD consistently outperforms both single-view self-distillation baselines and GRPO, achieving average Avg@8 gains of 3.1% and 2.2% over the strongest baselines on Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B, respectively. Moreover, on code-generation benchmarks (Codeforces, LiveCodeBench v6) using Qwen3-8B, AVSD outperforms the single-view self-distillation baseline by 2.4% on average.

</details>


### [158] [Design for Manufacturing: A Manufacturability Knowledge-Integrated Reinforcement Learning Framework for Free-Form Pipe Routing in Aeroengines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20644)
*Caicheng Wang,Zili Wang,Shuyou Zhang,Yongzhe Xiang,Zheyi Li,Liangyou Li,Jianrong Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Frenet框架下的管路路径优化方法（FPRO），将制造知识嵌入强化学习约束，实现航空发动机自由形态管路的设计-制造一体化，实验验证了其生成可制造、无碰撞路径的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前航空发动机管路设计脱离下游制造工艺，依赖人工试错迭代才能获得可制造方案，效率低下。亟需一种将制造知识融入设计过程的方法，实现设计与制造的一体化。

Method: 将管路路径建模为Frenet标架下的边值问题，用曲率和挠率轮廓（三次Hermite插值）表示路径；嵌入领域制造知识作为曲率/挠率的可行域约束；使用PPO算法（含随机探索和阶段引导奖励）优化路径；通过统一映射将优化路径转换为六轴自由弯曲机的弯曲模具运动轨迹。

Result: FPRO一致生成无碰撞、可制造的路径，几何轮廓比笛卡尔方法更平滑；相比先进RL基线，在终端对齐、路径长度、避障和可制造性方面收敛更快、性能更优；实际制造验证了数字设计与物理管路的几何一致性。

Conclusion: 将制造知识作为强化学习约束集成到Frenet框架中，可以有效桥接管路设计与制造之间的鸿沟，实现从设计到制造的直接映射，具有实际工程可行性。

Abstract: Design for manufacturing plays a critical role in advanced aeroengine development, where complex components necessitate careful consideration of manufacturability. However, current practices in pipe routing remain largely decoupled from down-stream manufacturing, leading to labor-intensive, trial-and-error iterations to achieve manufacturable designs. To address this problem, this study proposes the Frenet-based pipe routing optimization (FPRO) framework, a manufacturability knowledge-integrated reinforcement learning approach for free-form pipe design in aeroengines. FPRO formulates the routing problem as a boundary value problem in the Frenet frame. In this framework, the pipe path is represented by curvature and torsion profiles, which are generated using cubic Hermite interpolation. To integrate design and manufacturing, domain-specific manufacturing knowledge is embedded as constraints on the permissible ranges of curvature and torsion.
